71	- [ ] **TEST-01**: A rotation crash-safety/resume suite (the must-exist-before-merge suite) extends `tests/sdk-e2e` — the only real client→API IPNS publish/resolve round-trip — with abort-and-resume cases
72	- [x] **TEST-02**: The TS↔Rust AAD KAT is a single committed fixture asserted by both `packages/crypto/__tests__` and a Rust `#[test]` (a byte mismatch is silent total decryption failure)
73	- [ ] **TEST-03**: The winfsp read-path is validated via `Cargo Check & Test (Windows)` (authoritative) and the dispatch-gated desktop E2E is triggered explicitly
74	
75	## Future Requirements (deferred)
76	
77	### Capability layer (Tier 3)
78	
79	- **CAP-01**: Write-plane time-boxing / op-count caps (`ttl`/`opCap`/`capabilityId` on the grant row) — only meaningful on the write path, only if a mediated mechanism is ever chosen; read-side TTL is cryptographically unenforceable. Do NOT add to `Node`/`SealedChildRef`.
80	- **CAP-02**: Per-file "re-encrypt now" + `O(versions)` "purge history" for high-sensitivity content rotation
81	- **CAP-03**: Lazy rotation *walk* (rotate-on-next-write across a subtree) — the `rotateOne` primitive is amortizable later if the eager cost proves painful
82	
83	### Infra
84

88	
89	Explicitly excluded; documented to prevent scope creep.
90	
91	| Feature | Reason |
92	| --- | --- |
93	| Data migration / dual-codec bridge | Greenfield — no prod data, staging wiped; `node/v3` is the sole codec |
94	| Mediated write signing (`POST /ipns/sign`, approach a/d) | Runner-up only; (c) full Ed25519 rotation is ratified (ADR 0001); turns the untrusted relay into a signing oracle |
95	| Read-side TTL / op-caps | Cryptographically unenforceable — once a reader holds key + CID, IPFS serves it forever |
96	| Retroactive content protection | Read-revoke protects future content/navigation only; already-distributed CIDs + prior versions stay readable (ADR 0002) |
97	| Lazy rotation walk | Eager walk is the committed model this milestone |
98	| Network-first resolve repoint | Stays a post-v2.0 v2 move; near-term DB-canonical with generation + seq-floor authority |
99	| SEED-001 TEE cost cycling | Separable infra-cost optimization; deferred to a future infra milestone |
100	| Encrypted Productivity Suite | Deferred to a post-v2.0 milestone |

Shared `SharedFolderState` (`types.ts:205`) carries the full write-body model. Owned file write (a/b) needs either (i) extend the owned model to the write-body model like shared (clean copy of `shared-write.ts`, larger blast radius), or (ii) keep read-chain-only and thread the file `ipnsPrivateKey`/writeKey another way. The self-heal chokepoint `requireFolder`(:492) depends on it. **Critical path.** Primitives EXIST: pattern in `rotateReadFromNode` (`sdk-core/rotation/engine.ts:990-1014`) + `navigateReadChain`; write-key recovery via `shared-write.ts` `walkChildWriteKey`/`unsealParentWriteBody`.\n- `packages/sdk/src/client.ts:766` — `createFolder`: adapt `createSharedSubfolder` (`shared-write.ts:298`). Client seam: `buildSharedWriteContextFromState` (`client.ts:2095`) routes content pins via `pinWithMode` (BYO/external) but node metadata through CipherBox.\n\nZeroization (D-09): mint keys as `let x: Uint8Array | null`; `.fill(0)`+null on success AND in `catch`/`finally`; **never zero caller-supplied buffers** (a derived key is zeroed by the deriver, a caller-supplied one is not — see `moveInSharedFolder` `didDeriveWriteKey:843-864`).\n\n---\n\n## Validation Architecture\n\nThe phase's exit gate is the **full `tests/web-e2e` Playwright suite** (login→browse→upload→download→share→delete→versions→rotation UX) — this is the primary, integration-tier validation and is what the user requires to prove Phases 62–68 at runtime.\n\n- **Local/CI stack:** Postgres + Kubo (v0.42, CORS) + redis + mock-ipns-routing (`tools/mock-ipns-routing`); Playwright `webServer` auto-starts mock-ipns (:3001), API dev (:3000), web dev (:5173). Run: `pnpm test:web-e2e`. See `.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml` for the canonical recipe.\n- **New sdk-core owned-file write-chain (`createFileMetadata`/`updateFileMetadata`)** is genuine new logic → cover with **Vitest** in `packages/sdk-core` (analog: existing `shared-write`/rotation suites), TDD-eligible.\n- **SDK-tier round-trip:** the `tests/sdk-e2e` suite (real client→API IPNS) should stay green for any sdk-core write-chain change.\n- **SC#5 doctrine:** ZERO `apps/web/src/*.spec.ts` — web behavior proven only by web-e2e; testable logic hoisted to SDK.\n- **Backstop / non-inferable checks:** the `grep -rn \"not implemented — phase 6\"` emptiness assertion (SC#1); per-capability web-e2e specs already exist for most flows (upload, sharing, versions, recycle-bin, media, streaming, invite) — enable them by making the runtime real.\n"
}

NO new codec/crypto, NO change to the frozen `SealedChildRef`/`Node` read schema.\n- **Delete** `packages/sdk/src/reencrypt.ts` (obsolete, no callers).\n- ZERO new `apps/web/src/**/*.spec.ts` (SC#5). Web behavior proven ONLY by `tests/web-e2e` Playwright specs (most already exist — enabling them by making the runtime real is the point). Hoist testable logic to SDK (Vitest).\n- OUT OF SCOPE: FUSE/WinFsp (Phase 69); Tier-3 capability layer.\n- Zeroization (D-09): zero minted/derived keys on success + catch/finally; never zero caller-supplied buffers (see `shared-write.ts` `didDeriveWriteKey`).\n- Cross-package build order: after sdk-core/sdk edits, rebuild dist before web typecheck/e2e.\n</scope_fences>\n\n<downstream_consumer>\nOutput consumed by /gsd-execute-phase. Cover every capability group from RESEARCH (read a–d; write a–f). | Wave | Plans | What it builds |
|------|-------|----------------|
| 1 | 01 | Owned write-body foundation: `publishEmptyRootNode`, write-body in `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish`, `ensureFolderLoaded` recovery, `FolderState.writeKey` (**resolves D-03**) |
| 2 | 02, 03, 05, 07 | Owned `createFolder`+bin collectors+delete `reencrypt.ts`; login root-Node init; shared read nav (`navigateReadChain`); **[TDD] the one genuine build** — sdk-core owned file-node chain |
| 3 | 04, 08 | Owned file read services + `resolveKinds` kind-cache (**resolves D-02**); client shared-write wrappers |
| 4 | 06, 09, 10, 11, 14 | Owned read UI (preview/streaming); client owned file write; shared-folder write ops; sharing create+invite; **`resolveKinds` wiring into folder-load paths** (the revision fix) |
| 5 | 12 | Owned file write + versions web wiring |
| 6 | 13 | **web-e2e enablement + triage → full suite green (exit gate)** |

**Design decisions resolved (your review points):**
- **D-02 (file-vs-folder `kind`)** → resolve-and-cache at folder-load into an in-memory `kind-cache.ts`; `isFileRef` stays synchronous; **no change to the frozen `SealedChildRef` schema**.
- **D-03 (owned write model)** → extend owned `FolderState` to carry a write-body (forced: v3 `SealedChildRef` dropped `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted`, so the sealed write-body is the only schema-legal home for a recoverable subfolder key).

Confirmed — and yes, **merge Phase 68 first.** The facts back your instinct:

**1. It matches the established v2.0 model.** `main` already has Phases 61–67, each landed as its own squash PR (#578–#586). Phase 68 is the only one not yet on main (this branch is 91 commits ahead, main 0 behind).

**2. GSD requires it for 68.1.** `branching_strategy: phase` forks the next phase's branch off `origin/main`. If Phase 68 isn't on main, 68.1 would fork from a main that lacks it — losing the rotation UX, `ensureFolderLoaded` gating, `folderTree` refresh, and `rotationHighWater` that 68.1's plans build directly on.

**3. web-e2e does NOT block the PR.** I checked the CI:
- `ci.yml` = the PR gate (`pull_request → main`): lint/typecheck/unit — Phase 68 passes these.
- `ci-e2e.yml` (which calls web-e2e) runs **only on `push: [main]`** — i.e. *after* merge, not on PRs.
So Phase 68 can merge exactly as 62–67 did (all merged with these same stubs present since #578). web-e2e will run red on main post-merge (as it has all through v2.0) — and **68.1 is what turns it green.** That's the intended sequence, not a regression.
